  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ABSA使用分类极性标签存在局限性，需要扩展到效价-唤醒度(VA)维度建模以捕捉更丰富的情感信息。同时需要将ABSA从消费者评论扩展到公共议题讨论（如政治、能源、气候问题），为此引入维度立场分析(DimStance)。

Method: 任务分为两个赛道：Track A (DimABSA)包含三个子任务：(1)维度方面情感回归，(2)维度方面情感三元组提取，(3)维度方面情感四元组提取；Track B (DimStance)仅包含立场目标的回归子任务。引入连续F1(cF1)指标联合评估结构化提取和VA回归。

Result: 任务吸引了超过400名参与者，收到112份最终提交和42篇系统描述论文。报告了基线结果，分析了表现最佳的系统，并讨论了关键设计选择，为方面和立场目标级别的维度情感分析提供了见解。

Conclusion: 该共享任务成功推动了维度情感分析的发展，通过VA维度建模和连续评估指标改进了传统ABSA，并将应用范围扩展到公共议题讨论。所有资源已在GitHub仓库公开。

Abstract: We present the SemEval-2026 shared task on Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA), which improves traditional ABSA by modeling sentiment along valence-arousal (VA) dimensions rather than using categorical polarity labels. To extend ABSA beyond consumer reviews to public-issue discourse (e.g., political, energy, and climate issues), we introduce an additional task, Dimensional Stance Analysis (DimStance), which treats stance targets as aspects and reformulates stance detection as regression in the VA space. The task consists of two tracks: Track A (DimABSA) and Track B (DimStance). Track A includes three subtasks: (1) dimensional aspect sentiment regression, (2) dimensional aspect sentiment triplet extraction, and (3) dimensional aspect sentiment quadruplet extraction, while Track B includes only the regression subtask for stance targets. We also introduce a continuous F1 (cF1) metric to jointly evaluate structured extraction and VA regression. The task attracted more than 400 participants, resulting in 112 final submissions and 42 system description papers. We report baseline results, discuss top-performing systems, and analyze key design choices to provide insights into dimensional sentiment analysis at the aspect and stance-target levels. All resources are available on our GitHub repository.

</details>


### [151] [Is Cross-Lingual Transfer in Bilingual Models Human-Like? A Study with Overlapping Word Forms in Dutch and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07067)
*Iza Škrjanec,Irene Elisabeth Winther,Vera Demberg,Stefan L. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 双语语言模型中的跨语言激活：研究探讨了双语Transformer模型是否再现了人类双语阅读中的朋友词促进和假朋友词干扰模式，发现模型效果主要受词汇共享编码方式影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双语者在阅读时会出现跨语言激活现象，朋友词（cognates）通常产生促进效应，而假朋友词（interlingual homographs）则引起干扰或无效应。本研究旨在探究双语语言模型中的跨语言激活是否与人类双语者的这些模式相似。

Method: 训练荷兰语-英语因果Transformer模型，设置四种词汇共享条件来操纵（假）朋友词是否获得共享或语言特定的嵌入表示。使用双语阅读研究的心理语言学刺激材料，通过惊奇度（surprisal）和嵌入相似性分析评估模型。

Result: 模型在很大程度上保持语言分离，跨语言效应主要在嵌入共享时出现。在这种情况下，朋友词和假朋友词相对于控制词都显示出促进效应。回归分析表明这些效应主要由频率驱动，而非形式-意义映射的一致性。只有当朋友词共享嵌入时，才能再现双语者的定性模式。

Conclusion: 双语语言模型能够捕捉一些跨语言激活效应，但其与人类处理的对应关系关键取决于词汇重叠的编码方式，这可能限制了它们作为双语阅读模型的解释充分性。

Abstract: Bilingual speakers show cross-lingual activation during reading, especially for words with shared surface form. Cognates (friends) typically lead to facilitation, whereas interlingual homographs (false friends) cause interference or no effect. We examine whether cross-lingual activation in bilingual language models mirrors these patterns. We train Dutch-English causal Transformers under four vocabulary-sharing conditions that manipulate whether (false) friends receive shared or language-specific embeddings. Using psycholinguistic stimuli from bilingual reading studies, we evaluate the models through surprisal and embedding similarity analyses. The models largely maintain language separation, and cross-lingual effects arise primarily when embeddings are shared. In these cases, both friends and false friends show facilitation relative to controls. Regression analyses reveal that these effects are mainly driven by frequency rather than consistency in form-meaning mapping. Only when just friends share embeddings are the qualitative patterns of bilinguals reproduced. Overall, bilingual language models capture some cross-linguistic activation effects. However, their alignment with human processing seems to critically depend on how lexical overlap is encoded, possibly limiting their explanatory adequacy as models of bilingual reading.

</details>


### [152] [Multilingual Embedding Probes Fail to Generalize Across Learner Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07095)
*Laurits Lyngbaek,Ross Deans Kristensen-McLachlan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言嵌入模型是否编码了语言通用的熟练度表示？研究发现，虽然模型在分布内评估中能有效预测CEFR熟练度，但在跨语料库评估中性能崩溃，表明学习到的是语料库特定特征而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多语言嵌入模型是否编码了语言通用的熟练度表示，这对于基于表示的熟练度自适应语言技术具有重要意义。

Method: 在Qwen3-Embedding (0.6B, 4B, 8B)的隐藏状态激活上训练线性和非线性探针，预测九个语料库、七种语言中学习者文本的CEFR熟练度水平，并与基于表层文本特征的基线进行比较。

Result: 分布内评估中探针表现良好（QWK≈0.7），显著优于基线，中间层预测效果最佳；但跨语料库评估中所有探针类型和模型大小的性能都崩溃，残差分析显示探针趋向于预测均匀分布的标签。

Conclusion: 当前多语言嵌入模型并未直接编码语言通用的熟练度，学习到的映射捕获的是语料库特定的分布属性（主题、语言、任务类型、评分方法），而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度。

Abstract: Do multilingual embedding models encode a language-general representation of proficiency? We investigate this by training linear and non-linear probes on hidden-state activations from Qwen3-Embedding (0.6B, 4B, 8B) to predict CEFR proficiency levels from learner texts across nine corpora and seven languages. We compare five probing architectures against a baseline trained on surface-level text features. Under in-distribution evaluation, probes achieve strong performance ($QWK\approx0.7$), substantially outperforming the surface baseline, with middle layers consistently yielding the best predictions. However, in cross-corpus evaluation performance collapses across all probe types and model sizes. Residual analysis reveals that out-of-distribution probes converge towards predicting uniformly distributed labels, indicating that the learned mappings capture corpus-specific distributional properties (topic, language, task type, rating methodology) rather than an abstract, transferable proficiency dimension. These results suggest that current multilingual embeddings do not straightforwardly encode language-general proficiency, with implications for representation-based approaches to proficiency-adaptive language technology.

</details>


### [153] [STRIDE-ED: A Strategy-Grounded Stepwise Reasoning Framework for Empathetic Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07100)
*Hongru Ji,Yuyin Fan,Meng Zhao,Xianghua Li,Lianwei Wu,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STRIDE-ED：一个基于策略、可解释的深度推理框架，通过结构化、策略条件推理来建模共情对话，结合高质量数据构建和两阶段训练，在自动指标和人工评估中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共情对话方法存在三个根本限制：缺乏全面的共情策略框架、缺乏明确的任务对齐多阶段推理、缺乏高质量的策略感知数据，导致无法有效建模共情对话作为复杂的多阶段认知和决策过程。

Method: 1. 提出STRIDE-ED框架，通过结构化、策略条件推理建模共情对话；2. 开发策略感知数据精炼流水线，集成LLM标注、多模型一致性加权评估和动态采样；3. 采用两阶段训练范式：监督微调+多目标强化学习，使模型行为与目标情感、共情策略和响应格式对齐。

Result: 广泛实验表明，STRIDE-ED能够泛化到多种开源LLM，并在自动指标和人工评估中持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: STRIDE-ED通过结构化策略条件推理、高质量数据构建和两阶段训练，有效解决了共情对话建模中的关键挑战，为建模复杂的多阶段认知决策过程提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Empathetic dialogue requires not only recognizing a user's emotional state but also making strategy-aware, context-sensitive decisions throughout response generation. However, the lack of a comprehensive empathy strategy framework, explicit task-aligned multi-stage reasoning, and high-quality strategy-aware data fundamentally limits existing approaches, preventing them from effectively modeling empathetic dialogue as a complex, multi-stage cognitive and decision-making process. To address these challenges, we propose STRIDE-ED, a STRategy-grounded, Interpretable, and DEep reasoning framework that models Empathetic Dialogue through structured, strategy-conditioned reasoning. To support effective learning, we develop a strategy-aware data refinement pipeline integrating LLM-based annotation, multi-model consistency-weighted evaluation, and dynamic sampling to construct high-quality training data aligned with empathetic strategies. Furthermore, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm that combines supervised fine-tuning with multi-objective reinforcement learning to better align model behaviors with target emotions, empathetic strategies, and response formats. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STRIDE-ED generalizes across diverse open-source LLMs and consistently outperforms existing methods on both automatic metrics and human evaluations.

</details>


### [154] [The Impact of Steering Large Language Models with Persona Vectors in Educational Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07102)
*Yongchao Wu,Aron Henriksson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了激活导向的人格向量在教育场景中对大语言模型生成和评分的影响，发现人格导向会降低答案质量，且在开放性英语语言艺术任务中影响远大于事实性科学任务，评分方面存在可预测的校准偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于激活的导向可以在推理时个性化大语言模型，但其在教育环境中的效果尚不清楚。本研究旨在系统性地探究人格特质导向在短答案生成和自动评分中的影响，特别是在不同任务类型和模型架构下的表现差异。

Method: 研究在ASAP-SAS基准测试上，为七种人格特质创建人格向量，并在涵盖两种架构的三个模型上进行实验。分析人格导向对短答案生成质量和自动评分校准的影响，比较开放性英语语言艺术任务与事实性科学任务之间的差异，以及不同模型架构的敏感性。

Result: 人格导向总体上降低了答案质量，对开放性英语语言艺术任务的影响比对事实性科学任务大得多（解释性和论证性任务敏感性高达11倍）。评分方面观察到可预测的效价对齐校准偏移：邪恶和不礼貌的评分者评分更严格，而善良和乐观的评分者评分更宽松。英语语言艺术任务对评分者个性化的敏感性是科学任务的2.5-3倍，混合专家模型比密集模型显示出约6倍大的校准偏移。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统研究激活导向人格特质在教育生成和评分中影响的工作。结果表明，在教育环境中部署导向模型时，需要考虑任务感知和架构感知的校准，因为人格导向对模型行为的影响在不同任务类型和模型架构中存在显著差异。

Abstract: Activation-based steering can personalize large language models at inference time, but its effects in educational settings remain unclear. We study persona vectors for seven character traits in short-answer generation and automated scoring on the ASAP-SAS benchmark across three models spanning two architectures. Persona steering lowers answer quality overall, with much larger effects on open-ended English Language Arts (ELA) prompts than on factual science prompts; interpretive and argumentative tasks are up to 11x more sensitive. On the scoring side, we observe predictable valence-aligned calibration shifts: evil and impolite scorers grade more harshly, while good and optimistic scorers grade more leniently. ELA tasks are 2.5-3x more susceptible to scorer personalization than science tasks, and the Mixture-of-Experts model shows roughly 6x larger calibration shifts than the dense models. To our knowledge, this is the first study to systematically examine the effects of activation-steered persona traits in educational generation and scoring, and the results highlight the need for task-aware and architecture-aware calibration when deploying steered models in educational settings.

</details>


### [155] [Yale-DM-Lab at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Deterministic Grounding and Multi-Pass Evidence Alignment for EHR Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07116)
*Elyas Irankhah,Samah Fodeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 耶鲁-DM-Lab系统在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中采用多模型集成方法处理患者病历问答，包含四个子任务：问题重述、证据句识别、答案生成和证据-答案对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决患者对住院记录提问的自动处理问题，需要将患者口语化问题转化为临床医生可理解的专业问题，并准确识别相关证据、生成答案并进行对齐验证。

Method: 采用多模型集成策略：ST1使用Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o双模型管道进行问题重述；ST2-ST4使用Azure托管的模型集成（o3、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.1、DeepSeek-R1），结合少样本提示和投票策略。

Result: 在开发集上获得最佳分数：ST4微F1为88.81，ST2宏F1为65.72，ST3为34.01，ST1为33.05。发现模型多样性和集成投票能持续提升性能，对齐准确性主要受推理能力限制。

Conclusion: 多模型集成方法在医疗问答任务中有效，模型多样性是关键成功因素，未来工作需改进推理能力以提升对齐准确性。

Abstract: We describe the Yale-DM-Lab system for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task. The task studies patient-authored questions about hospitalization records and contains four subtasks (ST): clinician-interpreted question reformulation, evidence sentence identification, answer generation, and evidence-answer alignment. ST1 uses a dual-model pipeline with Claude Sonnet 4 and GPT-4o to reformulate patient questions into clinician-interpreted questions. ST2-ST4 rely on Azure-hosted model ensembles (o3, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.1, and DeepSeek-R1) combined with few-shot prompting and voting strategies. Our experiments show three main findings. First, model diversity and ensemble voting consistently improve performance compared to single-model baselines. Second, the full clinician answer paragraph is provided as additional prompt context for evidence alignment. Third, results on the development set show that alignment accuracy is mainly limited by reasoning. The best scores on the development set reach 88.81 micro F1 on ST4, 65.72 macro F1 on ST2, 34.01 on ST3, and 33.05 on ST1.

</details>


### [156] [Are Non-English Papers Reviewed Fairly? Language-of-Study Bias in NLP Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07119)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Abdulfattah Safa,Verena Blaschke,Erika Lombart,Marie-Catherine de Marneffe,Gözde Gül Şahin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统研究NLP同行评审中的语言研究偏见，区分正负偏见形式，创建LOBSTER数据集，分析15,645篇评审，发现非英语论文面临更高偏见率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审在NLP发表过程中起核心作用，但存在各种偏见。本研究关注语言研究偏见：评审人基于论文研究的语言而非科学价值进行不同评价。尽管评审指南明确禁止此类偏见，但对其理解不足。先前研究将其归为宽泛的弱评审类别，未将其定义为独特的偏见形式

Method: 1. 首次系统表征语言研究偏见，区分正负偏见形式；2. 创建人工标注数据集LOBSTER；3. 开发检测方法，达到87.37宏平均F1分数；4. 分析15,645篇评审，估计正负偏见如何随研究语言变化；5. 识别负面偏见的四个子类别

Result: 1. 非英语论文面临显著高于纯英语论文的偏见率；2. 负面偏见始终超过正面偏见；3. 识别出四个负面偏见子类别，其中要求不合理跨语言泛化是最主要形式；4. 检测方法达到87.37宏平均F1分数；5. 公开所有资源支持更公平的评审实践

Conclusion: 这是对NLP同行评审中语言研究偏见的首次系统研究，揭示了非英语论文面临的不公平评价问题。研究提供了检测方法和数据集，支持在NLP及其他领域建立更公平的评审实践。公开资源将促进相关研究发展

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in the NLP publication process, but is susceptible to various biases. Here, we study language-of-study (LoS) bias: the tendency for reviewers to evaluate a paper differently based on the language(s) it studies, rather than its scientific merit. Despite being explicitly flagged in reviewing guidelines, such biases are poorly understood. Prior work treats such comments as part of broader categories of weak or unconstructive reviews without defining them as a distinct form of bias. We present the first systematic characterization of LoS bias, distinguishing negative and positive forms, and introduce the human-annotated dataset LOBSTER (Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review) and a method achieving 87.37 macro F1 for detection. We analyze 15,645 reviews to estimate how negative and positive biases differ with respect to the LoS, and find that non-English papers face substantially higher bias rates than English-only ones, with negative bias consistently outweighing positive bias. Finally, we identify four subcategories of negative bias, and find that demanding unjustified cross-lingual generalization is the most dominant form. We publicly release all resources to support work on fairer reviewing practices in NLP and beyond.

</details>


### [157] [Language Bias under Conflicting Information in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07123)
*Robert Östling,Murathan Kurfalı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将"大海捞针"冲突信息范式扩展到多语言环境，发现所有测试的LLM（包括GPT-5.2）在大多数情况下都会忽略语言冲突，并自信地只给出一个可能的答案，且存在对俄语的普遍偏见和对中文的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究LLM在处理多语言冲突信息时是否存在语言偏见。先前研究表明LLM在整合冲突信息时存在偏见，本研究旨在验证这种偏见是否也存在于不同语言信息之间。

Method: 研究方法是将"大海捞针"冲突信息范式扩展到多语言设置，使用五种不同语言的自然新闻领域数据进行综合评估，测试了不同规模的多语言LLM。

Result: 研究发现：1）所有测试的LLM（包括GPT-5.2）在大多数情况下忽略冲突，自信地只给出一个可能的答案；2）存在一致的语言偏好偏见：普遍对俄语有偏见，在最长上下文长度下对中文有偏好；3）这种模式在中国境内和境外训练的模型中一致存在，但在前者中更为明显。

Conclusion: 结论是LLM在处理多语言冲突信息时确实存在系统性语言偏见，这种偏见在不同模型和训练环境中具有一致性，表明需要进一步研究多语言LLM的公平性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to contain biases in the process of integrating conflicting information when answering questions. Here we ask whether such biases also exist with respect to which language is used for each conflicting piece of information. To answer this question, we extend the conflicting needles in a haystack paradigm to a multilingual setting and perform a comprehensive set of evaluations with naturalistic news domain data in five different languages, for a range of multilingual LLMs of different sizes. We find that all LLMs tested, including GPT-5.2, ignore the conflict and confidently assert only one of the possible answers in the large majority of cases. Furthermore, there is a consistent bias across models in which languages are preferred, with a general bias against Russian and, for the longest context lengths, in favor of Chinese. Both of these patterns are consistent between models trained inside and outside of mainland China, though somewhat stronger in the former category.

</details>


### [158] [Dynamic Context Evolution for Scalable Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07147)
*Ryan Lingo,Rajeev Chhajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE)框架，通过三种机制解决大语言模型跨批次模式崩溃问题，显著提升输出多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在独立跨批次提示时会产生重复输出（跨批次模式崩溃），现有方法依赖临时去重和种子轮换，缺乏系统化框架

Method: 提出DCE框架包含三个机制：1) 语言化尾部采样（模型自我评估并丢弃明显想法）；2) 语义记忆（维护持久嵌入索引以拒绝跨批次近似重复）；3) 自适应提示演化（基于记忆状态和轮换多样性策略重构生成提示）

Result: 在三个领域（可持续包装概念、教育考试问题、创意写作提示）和两个模型家族（gpt-5-mini和claude-haiku-4-5）的实验中，DCE实现0.0%崩溃率（对比朴素提示的5.6%），产生17-18个HDBSCAN聚类（对比朴素方法的2-17个），表明概念结构更丰富

Conclusion: DCE有效解决跨批次模式崩溃问题，去重和提示演化单独使用不足但联合有效，成本约$0.50/千候选，无需微调或定制架构

Abstract: Large language models produce repetitive output when prompted independently across many batches, a phenomenon we term cross-batch mode collapse: the progressive loss of output diversity when a language model is prompted repeatedly without access to its prior generations. Practitioners have long mitigated this with ad hoc deduplication and seed rotation, but no principled framework exists. We introduce Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE), comprising three mechanisms: (1) verbalized tail sampling (the model labels each idea with a guess about how obvious it is, and obvious ideas are discarded), which filters high-probability candidates via model self-assessment; (2) semantic memory, which maintains a persistent embedding index to reject near-duplicates across batches; and (3) adaptive prompt evolution, which reconstructs the generation prompt each batch using memory state and rotating diversity strategies. In experiments across three domains (sustainable packaging concepts, educational exam questions, and creative writing prompts) and two model families (gpt-5-mini and claude-haiku-4-5), a component ablation across 2-3 random seeds per method shows that DCE achieves 0.0 +/- 0.0% collapse versus 5.6 +/- 2.0% for naive prompting, while producing 17-18 HDBSCAN clusters per seed versus naive's volatile 2-17, indicating reliably richer conceptual structure. These results are validated with an independent embedding model (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) and hold across sensitivity sweeps of the VTS threshold tau and dedup threshold delta. Deduplication and prompt evolution are individually insufficient but jointly effective, at approximately $0.50 per 1,000 candidates using only standard API calls, with no fine-tuning or custom architectures required.

</details>


### [159] [Agent-Driven Corpus Linguistics: A Framework for Autonomous Linguistic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07189)
*Jia Yu,Weiwei Yu,Pengfei Xiao,Fukun Xing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"智能体驱动的语料库语言学"，利用大语言模型通过结构化工具接口连接语料库查询引擎，自动完成假设生成、查询、结果解释和分析优化的完整研究循环，人类研究者仅需设定方向和评估最终输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语料库语言学依赖人类研究者进行假设制定、查询构建和结果解释，这一过程需要专业技术技能和大量时间。为降低技术门槛、提高研究效率，同时保持实证基础，需要一种能够自动化研究过程但仍以可验证语料证据为基础的方法。

Method: 开发了一个智能体驱动的语料库语言学框架，将大语言模型通过结构化工具使用接口连接到语料库查询引擎。使用模型上下文协议（MCP）将LLM智能体连接到CQP索引的古登堡语料库（500万词）。智能体自动执行完整的研究循环：生成假设、查询语料库、解释结果、多轮优化分析。

Result: 仅给定"调查英语强化词"的指令，智能体识别出历时性接力链（so+ADJ > very > really）、三种语义变化路径（去词汇化、极性固定、隐喻约束）以及语域敏感分布。控制实验表明，语料库基础提供了模型无法仅从训练数据产生的量化和可证伪性。在CLMET语料库（4000万词）上成功复制了Claridge（2025）和De Smet（2013）的两项已发表研究，定量结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的语料库研究能够以机器速度产生基于实证的研究发现，同时保持可验证的语料证据基础。这不仅降低了技术门槛，使更广泛的研究者能够进行语料库研究，而且为语料库语言学提供了新的方法论维度——关注谁进行研究，而非理论与数据的认识论关系。

Abstract: Corpus linguistics has traditionally relied on human researchers to formulate hypotheses, construct queries, and interpret results - a process demanding specialized technical skills and considerable time. We propose Agent-Driven Corpus Linguistics, an approach in which a large language model (LLM), connected to a corpus query engine via a structured tool-use interface, takes over the investigative cycle: generating hypotheses, querying the corpus, interpreting results, and refining analysis across multiple rounds. The human researcher sets direction and evaluates final output. Unlike unconstrained LLM generation, every finding is anchored in verifiable corpus evidence. We treat this not as a replacement for the corpus-based/corpus-driven distinction but as a complementary dimension: it concerns who conducts the inquiry, not the epistemological relationship between theory and data. We demonstrate the framework by linking an LLM agent to a CQP-indexed Gutenberg corpus (5 million tokens) via the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Given only "investigate English intensifiers," the agent identified a diachronic relay chain (so+ADJ > very > really), three pathways of semantic change (delexicalization, polarity fixation, metaphorical constraint), and register-sensitive distributions. A controlled baseline experiment shows that corpus grounding contributes quantification and falsifiability that the model cannot produce from training data alone. To test external validity, the agent replicated two published studies on the CLMET corpus (40 million tokens) - Claridge (2025) and De Smet (2013) - with close quantitative agreement. Agent-driven corpus research can thus produce empirically grounded findings at machine speed, lowering the technical barrier for a broader range of researchers.

</details>


### [160] [Efficient Learned Data Compression via Dual-Stream Feature Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07239)
*Huidong Ma,Xinyan Shi,Hui Sun,Xiaofei Yue,Xiaoguang Liu,Gang Wang,Wentong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双流多尺度解耦器与并发流并行流水线，解决学习型数据压缩中概率建模与系统效率的平衡问题，实现压缩率与吞吐量的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型数据压缩虽然取得了优越的压缩比，但平衡精确概率建模与系统效率仍然具有挑战性。传统单流架构难以同时捕获微观句法和宏观语义特征，需要深度串行堆叠导致延迟增加。异构系统受限于设备速度不匹配，串行处理导致吞吐量受阿姆达尔定律限制。

Method: 提出双流多尺度解耦器，将局部和全局上下文解耦，用浅层并行流替代深度串行处理；引入分层门控细化器进行自适应特征细化和精确概率建模；设计并发流并行流水线，克服系统瓶颈实现全流水线并行。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在压缩比和吞吐量方面均达到最先进性能，同时保持最低延迟和内存使用。

Conclusion: 通过解耦局部与全局上下文、采用并行处理架构，成功解决了学习型数据压缩中的效率瓶颈问题，实现了压缩性能与系统效率的平衡优化。

Abstract: While Learned Data Compression (LDC) has achieved superior compression ratios, balancing precise probability modeling with system efficiency remains challenging. Crucially, uniform single-stream architectures struggle to simultaneously capture micro-syntactic and macro-semantic features, necessitating deep serial stacking that exacerbates latency. Compounding this, heterogeneous systems are constrained by device speed mismatches, where throughput is capped by Amdahl's Law due to serial processing. To this end, we propose a Dual-Stream Multi-Scale Decoupler that disentangles local and global contexts to replace deep serial processing with shallow parallel streams, and incorporate a Hierarchical Gated Refiner for adaptive feature refinement and precise probability modeling. Furthermore, we design a Concurrent Stream-Parallel Pipeline, which overcomes systemic bottlenecks to achieve full-pipeline parallelism. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both compression ratio and throughput, while maintaining the lowest latency and memory usage. The code is available at https://github.com/huidong-ma/FADE.

</details>


### [161] [ClickGuard: A Trustworthy Adaptive Fusion Framework for Clickbait Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07272)
*Chhavi Dhiman,Naman Chawla,Riya Dhami,Gaurav Kumar,Ganesh Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClickGuard是一个用于点击诱饵检测的可信自适应融合框架，结合BERT嵌入和结构特征，通过语法-语义自适应融合块实现动态集成，在测试中达到96.93%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点击诱饵标题的广泛使用对在线可信度构成重大挑战，这些标题采用耸人听闻、误导性声明和模糊语言，需要有效的检测方法来确保可信的数字内容。

Method: 提出ClickGuard框架，结合BERT嵌入和结构特征，使用语法-语义自适应融合块(SSAFB)进行动态集成，采用混合CNN-BiLSTM架构捕获模式和依赖关系。

Result: 模型达到96.93%的测试准确率，优于最先进方法。通过LIME和排列特征重要性(PFI)评估可信度，消融研究验证了SSAFB在优化特征融合方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ClickGuard框架在多样化数据集上表现出稳健性能，通过解决语法-语义建模挑战，为增强在线内容可信度提供了可扩展、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread use of clickbait headlines, crafted to mislead and maximize engagement, poses a significant challenge to online credibility. These headlines employ sensationalism, misleading claims, and vague language, underscoring the need for effective detection to ensure trustworthy digital content. The paper introduces, ClickGuard: a trustworthy adaptive fusion framework for clickbait detection. It combines BERT embeddings and structural features using a Syntactic-Semantic Adaptive Fusion Block (SSAFB) for dynamic integration. The framework incorporates a hybrid CNN-BiLSTM to capture patterns and dependencies. The model achieved 96.93% testing accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. The model's trustworthiness is evaluated using LIME and Permutation Feature Importance (PFI) for interpretability and perturbation analysis. These methods assess the model's robustness and sensitivity to feature changes by measuring the average prediction variation. Ablation studies validated the SSAFB's effectiveness in optimizing feature fusion. The model demonstrated robust performance across diverse datasets, providing a scalable, reliable solution for enhancing online content credibility by addressing syntactic-semantic modelling challenges. Code of the work is available at: https://github.com/palindromeRice/ClickBait_Detection_Architecture

</details>


### [162] [Why teaching resists automation in an AI-inundated era: Human judgment, non-modular work, and the limits of delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07285)
*Songhee Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文反驳了"教学可被AI自动化"的观点，认为教学本质上是解释性、关系性和基于专业判断的工作，难以被技术完全取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前教育领域关于人工智能可以自动化教学的争论，作者认为这种观点错误地将教学视为模块化和程序化的工作，忽视了教学实践中的不可分割性。

Method: 基于近期文献和大型语言模型、检索增强生成系统的实证研究，从理论层面分析教学工作的本质特征，论证其抵抗自动化的原因。

Result: 教学工作的价值来源于对学习者、情境和关系的持续情境化解释，这些要素无法被完全规范、预测或建模。AI可以支持某些有限功能，但不能替代教学所需的人类判断和关系责任。

Conclusion: 只要教育实践依赖于对人类认知和学习的新兴理解，教学就仍然是一种抵抗自动化的专业工作。AI可以改善信息获取和支持特定教学活动，但不能消除有效教学所需的人类判断和关系责任。

Abstract: Debates about artificial intelligence (AI) in education often portray teaching as a modular and procedural job that can increasingly be automated or delegated to technology. This brief communication paper argues that such claims depend on treating teaching as more separable than it is in practice. Drawing on recent literature and empirical studies of large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems, I argue that although AI can support some bounded functions, instructional work remains difficult to automate in meaningful ways because it is inherently interpretive, relational, and grounded in professional judgment. More fundamentally, teaching and learning are shaped by human cognition, behavior, motivation, and social interaction in ways that cannot be fully specified, predicted, or exhaustively modeled. Tasks that may appear separable in principle derive their instructional value in practice from ongoing contextual interpretation across learners, situations, and relationships. As long as educational practice relies on emergent understanding of human cognition and learning, teaching remains a form of professional work that resists automation. AI may improve access to information and support selected instructional activities, but it does not remove the need for human judgment and relational accountability that effective teaching requires.

</details>


### [163] [Evaluating In-Context Translation with Synchronous Context-Free Grammar Transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07320)
*Jackson Petty,Jaulie Goe,Tal Linzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在低资源语言翻译中面临数据依赖问题，研究通过形式语法任务测试LLMs能否利用上下文语法描述进行翻译，发现语法规模、句子长度、形态差异和书写系统差异显著影响翻译准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言翻译需要大量训练数据，但LLMs可能通过上下文语言描述（如教科书、词典）来弥补数据不足。本研究旨在测试LLMs是否能够根据上下文提供的语法描述进行语言翻译，特别是能否推断语法描述与句子之间的关系。

Method: 采用形式化类比任务：基于上下文提供的同步上下文无关语法进行字符串转换。构建同步上下文无关语法来定义形式语言对，模拟自然语言的语法、形态和书写特征。在给定语法和源语言句子的条件下，测量LLMs将句子从一个形式语言翻译到另一个形式语言的能力。变量包括：语法规模、句子长度、语言句法和形态特性、书写系统。

Result: 1. LLMs翻译准确率随语法规模和句子长度增加而显著下降；2. 源语言和目标语言之间的形态差异和书写系统差异会严重降低模型性能；3. 错误分析显示模型主要倾向于：回忆错误的目标语言词汇、产生幻觉新词、或未翻译源语言词汇。

Conclusion: LLMs在利用上下文语法描述进行翻译方面存在局限性，特别是在处理复杂语法结构、长句以及语言间形态和书写差异时表现不佳。这表明当前LLMs在低资源语言翻译中依赖上下文语法描述的能力有限，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Low-resource languages pose a challenge for machine translation with large language models (LLMs), which require large amounts of training data. One potential way to circumvent this data dependence is to rely on LLMs' ability to use in-context descriptions of languages, like textbooks and dictionaries. To do so, LLMs must be able to infer the link between the languages' grammatical descriptions and the sentences in question. Here we isolate this skill using a formal analogue of the task: string transduction based on a formal grammar provided in-context. We construct synchronous context-free grammars which define pairs of formal languages designed to model particular aspects of natural language grammar, morphology, and written representation. Using these grammars, we measure how well LLMs can translate sentences from one formal language into another when given both the grammar and the source-language sentence. We vary the size of the grammar, the lengths of the sentences, the syntactic and morphological properties of the languages, and their written script. We note three key findings. First, LLMs' translation accuracy decreases markedly as a function of grammar size and sentence length. Second, differences in morphology and written representation between the source and target languages can strongly diminish model performance. Third, we examine the types of errors committed by models and find they are most prone to recall the wrong words from the target language vocabulary, hallucinate new words, or leave source-language words untranslated.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [EviSnap: Faithful Evidence-Cited Explanations for Cold-Start Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06172)
*Yingjun Dai,Ahmed El-Roby*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EviSnap是一个轻量级的冷启动跨域推荐框架，通过构建时解释的、基于证据引用的忠实推理来预测用户偏好，在性能优于现有方法的同时提供可审计的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域推荐系统要么使用不透明的嵌入映射，要么依赖难以审计的后处理或LLM生成的推理。需要一种能够提供忠实、可验证解释的推荐框架。

Method: 1. 使用LLM离线将嘈杂评论提炼为紧凑的方面卡片，每个方面都配有原文支持句子；2. 通过聚类方面嵌入诱导共享的领域无关概念库；3. 通过证据加权池化计算用户正面、负面和物品存在概念激活；4. 使用单一线性概念到概念映射跨域转移用户；5. 线性评分头产生每个概念的加性贡献，支持精确的分数分解和基于引用句子的反事实编辑。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews数据集上，跨越图书、电影和音乐的六个转移实验中，EviSnap始终优于强大的映射和评论文本基线方法，同时通过了基于删除和充分性测试的解释忠实性验证。

Conclusion: EviSnap提供了一个轻量级、高性能的跨域推荐框架，其预测通过构建时解释的、基于证据引用的忠实推理来实现，实现了可审计性和性能的平衡。

Abstract: Cold-start cross-domain recommender (CDR) systems predict a user's preferences in a target domain using only their source-domain behavior, yet existing CDR models either map opaque embeddings or rely on post-hoc or LLM-generated rationales that are hard to audit. We introduce EviSnap a lightweight CDR framework whose predictions are explained by construction with evidence-cited, faithful rationales. EviSnap distills noisy reviews into compact facet cards using an LLM offline, pairing each facet with verbatim supporting sentences. It then induces a shared, domain-agnostic concept bank by clustering facet embeddings and computes user-positive, user-negative, and item-presence concept activations via evidence-weighted pooling. A single linear concept-to-concept map transfers users across domains, and a linear scoring head yields per-concept additive contributions, enabling exact score decompositions and counterfactual 'what-if' edits grounded in the cited sentences. Experiments on the Amazon Reviews dataset across six transfers among Books, Movies, and Music show that EviSnap consistently outperforms strong mapping and review-text baselines while passing deletion- and sufficiency-based tests for explanation faithfulness.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Case Law: Evaluating Structure-Aware Retrieval and Safety in Statute-Centric Legal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06173)
*Kyubyung Chae,Jewon Yeom,Jeongjae Park,Seunghyun Bae,Ijun Jang,Hyunbin Jin,Jinkwan Jang,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SearchFireSafety是一个针对法规为中心的法律QA基准测试，专注于消防法规领域，评估模型在分层碎片化证据检索和上下文不足时安全拒绝的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律QA基准主要关注判例法，忽视了法规为中心监管推理的独特挑战。在法规领域，相关证据分布在分层链接的文档中，存在法规检索缺口，传统检索器失败且模型在上下文不完整时容易产生幻觉。

Method: 引入SearchFireSafety基准，以消防法规为代表性案例，采用双源评估框架：结合需要引用感知检索的真实世界问题和测试幻觉与拒绝行为的合成部分上下文场景。实验使用图引导检索方法。

Result: 实验显示图引导检索显著提高性能，但也揭示了关键的安全权衡：领域适应模型在关键法规证据缺失时更容易产生幻觉。模型在分层检索方面表现改善，但在安全拒绝方面存在不足。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要能够联合评估分层检索和模型安全性的基准测试，特别是在法规为中心的监管环境中。SearchFireSafety为这一需求提供了具体实现。

Abstract: Legal QA benchmarks have predominantly focused on case law, overlooking the unique challenges of statute-centric regulatory reasoning. In statutory domains, relevant evidence is distributed across hierarchically linked documents, creating a statutory retrieval gap where conventional retrievers fail and models often hallucinate under incomplete context. We introduce SearchFireSafety, a structure- and safety-aware benchmark for statute-centric legal QA. Instantiated on fire-safety regulations as a representative case, the benchmark evaluates whether models can retrieve hierarchically fragmented evidence and safely abstain when statutory context is insufficient. SearchFireSafety adopts a dual-source evaluation framework combining real-world questions that require citation-aware retrieval and synthetic partial-context scenarios that stress-test hallucination and refusal behavior. Experiments across multiple large language models show that graph-guided retrieval substantially improves performance, but also reveal a critical safety trade-off: domain-adapted models are more likely to hallucinate when key statutory evidence is missing. Our findings highlight the need for benchmarks that jointly evaluate hierarchical retrieval and model safety in statute-centric regulatory settings.

</details>


### [166] [Robustness Risk of Conversational Retrieval: Identifying and Mitigating Noise Sensitivity in Qwen3-Embedding Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06176)
*Weishu Chen,Zhouhui Hou,Mingjie Zhan,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现Qwen3-embedding模型在对话式检索中存在鲁棒性漏洞：未使用查询提示时，结构化对话噪声会过度检索并侵入排名前列，尽管语义上无信息价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索嵌入检索在现实对话场景下的鲁棒性问题，特别是当查询简短、对话式且定义不明确，而检索语料包含结构化对话内容时。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，聚焦Qwen3-embedding模型，在对话式检索设置下评估模型性能，比较不同模型规模、早期Qwen变体及其他密集检索基线，并测试轻量级查询提示的效果。

Result: 发现部署相关的鲁棒性漏洞：在未使用查询提示的对话检索中，结构化对话噪声会不成比例地被检索并进入排名前列；这一现象在不同模型规模中一致存在，在标准干净查询基准测试中基本不可见，且在Qwen3中比早期Qwen变体和其他基线更为显著；轻量级查询提示能有效抑制噪声侵入并恢复排名稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了对话检索中未被充分探索的鲁棒性风险，强调了评估协议需要反映部署系统复杂性的重要性，并表明查询提示是缓解噪声侵入的有效策略。

Abstract: We present an empirical study of embedding-based retrieval under realistic conversational settings, where queries are short, dialogue-like, and weakly specified, and retrieval corpora contain structured conversational artifacts. Focusing on Qwen3-embedding models, we identify a deployment-relevant robustness vulnerability: under conversational retrieval without query prompting, structured dialogue-style noise can become disproportionately retrievable and intrude into top-ranked results, despite being semantically uninformative. This failure mode emerges consistently across model scales, remains largely invisible under standard clean-query benchmarks, and is significantly more pronounced in Qwen3 than in earlier Qwen variants and other widely used dense retrieval baselines. We further show that lightweight query prompting qualitatively alters retrieval behavior, effectively suppressing noise intrusion and restoring ranking stability. Our findings highlight an underexplored robustness risk in conversational retrieval and underscore the importance of evaluation protocols that reflect the complexities of deployed systems.

</details>


### [167] [ARIA: Adaptive Retrieval Intelligence Assistant -- A Multimodal RAG Framework for Domain-Specific Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06179)
*Yue Luo,Dibakar Roy Sarkar,Rachel Herring Sangree,Somdatta Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ARIA是一个基于检索增强生成(RAG)的自适应检索智能助手框架，专门为大学课程创建智能教学助手，通过多模态内容提取和语义嵌入技术解决LLM在专业教育应用中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在专业教育应用中存在幻觉、知识更新有限和缺乏领域专业知识等显著限制，微调需要完整模型重训练导致巨大计算开销，而通用LLM在专业上下文中经常提供不准确响应。需要开发有效的领域特定教育支持系统来推进AI在教育中的应用。

Method: 提出ARIA框架，采用检索增强生成(RAG)架构，结合多模态内容提取管道：Docling用于结构化文档分析，Nougat用于数学公式识别，GPT-4 Vision API用于图表解释，使用e5-large-v2嵌入模型实现高语义性能和低延迟。通过工程化提示和响应控制保持教学一致性。

Result: 在约翰霍普金斯大学土木工程二年级课程"静力学与材料力学"的讲座材料上进行评估，与ChatGPT-5对比。结果显示：领域特定问题过滤准确率97.5%，正确回答了所有20个相关课程问题，拒绝了60个非相关查询中的58个，达到90.9%精确度、100%召回率和4.89/5.0的平均响应质量。

Conclusion: ARIA的课程无关架构代表了领域特定教育AI部署的可扩展框架，能够准确处理复杂教育材料，在专业教育应用中表现出优越的教学性能。

Abstract: Developing effective, domain-specific educational support systems is central to advancing AI in education. Although large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities, they face significant limitations in specialized educational applications, including hallucinations, limited knowledge updates, and lack of domain expertise. Fine-tuning requires complete model retraining, creating substantial computational overhead, while general-purpose LLMs often provide inaccurate responses in specialized contexts due to reliance on generalized training data. To address this, we propose ARIA (Adaptive Retrieval Intelligence Assistant), a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework for creating intelligent teaching assistants across university-level courses. ARIA leverages a multimodal content extraction pipeline combining Docling for structured document analysis, Nougat for mathematical formula recognition, and GPT-4 Vision API for diagram interpretation, with the e5-large-v2 embedding model for high semantic performance and low latency. This enables accurate processing of complex educational materials while maintaining pedagogical consistency through engineered prompts and response controls. We evaluate ARIA using lecture material from Statics and Mechanics of Materials, a sophomore-level civil engineering course at Johns Hopkins University, benchmarking against ChatGPT-5. Results demonstrate 97.5% accuracy in domain-specific question filtering and superior pedagogical performance. ARIA correctly answered all 20 relevant course questions while rejecting 58 of 60 non-relevant queries, achieving 90.9% precision, 100% recall, and 4.89/5.0 average response quality. These findings demonstrate that ARIA's course-agnostic architecture represents a scalable framework for domain-specific educational AI deployment.

</details>


### [168] [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Data for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06420)
*Youssef Abdou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究了推荐系统中训练数据规模对离线性能的影响，发现在典型用户-物品交互数据上，增加训练数据通常持续提升性能，未观察到明显的饱和点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中收集、存储和处理大规模交互数据在时间、能量和计算方面的成本越来越高，但尚不清楚何时额外数据不再提供有意义的增益。本研究旨在探究离线推荐性能如何随训练数据集规模增长而变化，以及是否能观察到饱和点。

Method: 采用可复现的Python评估流程，使用LensKit和RecBole两个工具包，包含11个至少700万交互的大规模公共数据集，评估10种工具-算法组合。使用绝对分层用户采样，在9个样本规模（从10万到1亿交互）上训练模型，并测量NDCG@10。通过组内最小-最大归一化使结果组可比，并对每个组的最后10-30%进行后期斜率分析。

Result: 原始NDCG通常随样本规模增加而增加，未观察到饱和点。归一化后显示明显正向趋势：在最大完成样本规模处，约75%的点也达到了该组的最佳观测性能。后期斜率分析进一步支持这一上升趋势：四分位距完全非负，中位数接近1.0。

Conclusion: 对于传统推荐系统在典型用户-物品交互数据上，纳入更多训练数据仍然主要是有益的，而较弱的缩放行为集中在非典型数据集案例和算法异常值（在本设置下的RecBole BPR）中。

Abstract: In recommender systems, collecting, storing, and processing large-scale interaction data is increasingly costly in terms of time, energy, and computation, yet it remains unclear when additional data stops providing meaningful gains. This paper investigates how offline recommendation performance evolves as the size of the training dataset increases and whether a saturation point can be observed. We implemented a reproducible Python evaluation workflow with two established toolkits, LensKit and RecBole, included 11 large public datasets with at least 7 million interactions, and evaluated 10 tool-algorithm combinations. Using absolute stratified user sampling, we trained models on nine sample sizes from 100,000 to 100,000,000 interactions and measured NDCG@10. Overall, raw NDCG usually increased with sample size, with no observable saturation point. To make result groups comparable, we applied min-max normalization within each group, revealing a clear positive trend in which around 75% of the points at the largest completed sample size also achieved the group's best observed performance. A late-stage slope analysis over the final 10-30% of each group further supported this upward trend: the interquartile range remained entirely non-negative with a median near 1.0. In summary, for traditional recommender systems on typical user-item interaction data, incorporating more training data remains primarily beneficial, while weaker scaling behavior is concentrated in atypical dataset cases and in the algorithmic outlier RecBole BPR under our setup.

</details>


### [169] [CASE: Cadence-Aware Set Encoding for Large-Scale Next Basket Repurchase Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06718)
*Yanan Cao,Ashish Ranjan,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CASE模型通过解耦商品级购买节奏学习和跨商品交互，实现显式日历时间建模，在保持生产可扩展性的同时提升复购推荐性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一篮子复购推荐模型将历史表示为按访问顺序索引的离散篮子事件序列，无法显式建模日历时间流逝或随着购买间隔天数更新商品排名，而购买节奏是零售推荐中的重要信号

Method: CASE将每个商品的购买历史表示为固定时间范围内的日历时间信号，应用共享多尺度时间卷积捕捉重复节奏，使用诱导集合注意力建模跨商品依赖关系，具有次二次复杂度

Result: 在三个公共基准测试和一个专有数据集上，CASE在多个截断点持续改进精确率、召回率和NDCG；在生产规模评估中，对千万级用户和大型商品目录，CASE在top-5上实现8.6%相对精确率和9.9%召回率提升

Conclusion: 可扩展的节奏感知建模在基准测试和工业环境中都能带来可衡量的性能提升，CASE通过显式日历时间建模有效捕捉复购行为的时间模式

Abstract: Repurchase behavior is a primary signal in large-scale retail recommendation, particularly in categories with frequent replenishment: many items in a user's next basket were previously purchased and their timing follows stable, item-specific cadences. Yet most next basket repurchase recommendation models represent history as a sequence of discrete basket events indexed by visit order, which cannot explicitly model elapsed calendar time or update item rankings as days pass between purchases. We present CASE (Cadence-Aware Set Encoding for next basket repurchase recommendation), which decouples item-level cadence learning from cross-item interaction, enabling explicit calendar-time modeling while remaining production-scalable. CASE represents each item's purchase history as a calendar-time signal over a fixed horizon, applies shared multi-scale temporal convolutions to capture recurring rhythms, and uses induced set attention to model cross-item dependencies with sub-quadratic complexity, allowing efficient batch inference at scale. Across three public benchmarks and a proprietary dataset, CASE consistently improves Precision, Recall, and NDCG at multiple cutoffs compared to strong next basket prediction baselines. In a production-scale evaluation with tens of millions of users and a large item catalog, CASE achieves up to 8.6% relative Precision and 9.9% Recall lift at top-5, demonstrating that scalable cadence-aware modeling yields measurable gains in both benchmark and industrial settings.

</details>


### [170] [Leveraging LLMs and Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs for Persona-Driven Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06928)
*Muskan Gupta,Suraj Thapa,Jyotsana Khatri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于知识图谱的隐式用户画像建模框架，用于解决会话推荐系统中匿名会话限制个性化的问题，通过两阶段架构整合LLM增强的项目表示和KG驱动的用户画像学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统会话推荐系统假设匿名会话限制了个性化能力，特别是在稀疏或冷启动条件下。现有LLM增强的推荐方法能生成丰富的项目表示，但难以在匿名会话中建模用户画像。需要一种方法将结构化关系信号与LLM能力结合，提升个性化推荐效果。

Method: 采用两阶段架构：1)个性化信息提取阶段：构建包含用户-项目、项目-项目、项目-特征关联和DBpedia元数据的异构知识图谱，使用HDGI目标在LLM初始化的项目嵌入上无监督学习隐式用户画像；2)个性化信息利用阶段：将学到的画像表示与LLM项目嵌入整合到改进的数据驱动SBRS架构中，生成候选项目集，再用基础序列模型重排序以强调短期会话意图。

Result: 在Amazon Books和Amazon Movies & TV数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比仅使用会话历史推导用户嵌入的序列模型有持续改进，验证了KG驱动的用户画像建模在会话推荐中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过将用户画像建模基于知识图谱的结构化关系信号，解决了匿名会话中的个性化限制问题，为会话推荐系统提供了更有效的个性化方法，特别是在稀疏和冷启动场景下。

Abstract: Session-based recommendation systems (SBRS) aim to capture user's short-term intent from interaction sequences. However, the common assumption of anonymous sessions limits personalization, particularly under sparse or cold-start conditions. Recent advances in LLM-augmented recommendation have shown that LLMs can generate rich item representations, but modeling user personas with LLMs remains challenging due to anonymous sessions. In this work, we propose a persona-driven SBRS framework that explicitly models latent user personas inferred from a heterogeneous knowledge graph (KG) and integrates them into a data-driven recommendation pipeline.Our framework adopts a two-stage architecture consisting of personalized information extraction and personalized information utilization, inspired by recent chain-of-thought recommendation approaches. In the personalized information extraction stage, we construct a heterogeneous KG that integrates time-independent user-item, item-item, item-feature association, and metadata from DBpedia. We then learn latent user personas in an unsupervised manner using a Heterogeneous Deep Graph Infomax (HDGI) objective over a KG initialized with LLM-derived item embeddings. In the personalized information utilization stage, the learned persona representations together with LLM-derived item embeddings are incorporated into a modified architecture of data-driven SBRS to generate a candidate set of relevant items, followed by reranking using the base sequential model to emphasize short-term session intent. Unlike prior approaches that rely solely on sequence modeling or text-based user representations, our method grounds user persona modeling in structured relational signals derived from a KG. Experiments on Amazon Books and Amazon Movies & TV demonstrate that our approach consistently improves over sequential models with user embeddings derived using session history.

</details>


### [171] [MARVEL: Multimodal Adaptive Reasoning-intensiVe Expand-rerank and retrievaL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07079)
*Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Mohamed Mahmoud,Mostafa Farouk Senussi,Mahmoud Abdalla,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MARVEL是一个统一的多模态检索框架，通过LLM驱动的查询扩展、推理增强的密集检索器和GPT-4o链式思维重排序，在MM-BRIGHT基准上实现37.9 nDCG@10，比最佳多模态编码器提升10.3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索面临根本性挑战：最佳视觉语言编码器在推理密集型多模态检索基准MM-BRIGHT上仅达到27.6 nDCG@10，表现不如强文本系统。现有方法仅孤立地处理三个关键能力：扩展查询潜在意图、使用复杂推理训练的模型进行检索、通过显式逐步推理对候选结果重排序。

Method: 提出MARVEL统一流水线：1) LLM驱动的查询扩展；2) MARVEL-Retriever - 针对复杂多模态查询微调的推理增强密集检索器；3) GPT-4o链式思维重排序，可选多通道互惠排名融合。

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT的29个技术领域评估中，MARVEL达到37.9 nDCG@10，比最佳多模态编码器提升+10.3分，在27/29领域超越所有单阶段基线，在剩余两个高度专业化领域（加密、量子计算）匹配或接近最佳基线。

Conclusion: 推理密集型多模态检索最好通过统一的扩展-检索-重排序框架解决，MARVEL证明了这种集成方法的有效性，在多个技术领域显著提升检索性能。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval over text corpora remains a fundamental challenge: the best vision-language encoder achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, a reasoning-intensive multimodal retrieval benchmark, underperforming strong text-only systems. We argue that effective multimodal retrieval requires three tightly integrated capabilities that existing approaches address only in isolation: expanding the query's latent intent, retrieving with a model trained for complex reasoning, and reranking via explicit step-by-step reasoning over candidates. We introduce \textbf{MARVEL} (\textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}easoning-intensi\textbf{V}e \textbf{E}xpand-rerank and retrieva\textbf{L}), a unified pipeline that combines LLM-driven query expansion, \textbf{MARVEL-Retriever} -- a reasoning-enhanced dense retriever fine-tuned for complex multimodal queries -- and GPT-4o-based chain-of-thought reranking with optional multi-pass reciprocal rank fusion. Evaluated on MM-BRIGHT across 29 technical domains, MARVEL achieves \textbf{37.9} nDCG@10, surpassing the best multimodal encoder by \textbf{+10.3 points} and outperforming all single-stage baselines in 27 of 29 domains and matching or approaching the best baseline in the remaining two highly-specialized domains (Crypto, Quantum Computing), demonstrating that reasoning-intensive multimodal retrieval is best addressed through a unified expand-retrieve-rerank framework. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


### [172] [Leveraging Artist Catalogs for Cold-Start Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07090)
*Yan-Martin Tamm,Gregor Meehan,Vojtěch Nekl,Vojtěch Vančura,Rodrigo Alves,Johan Pauwels,Anna Aljanaki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出ACARec模型，通过利用艺术家级别的协作信号解决音乐推荐中的物品冷启动问题，相比仅使用内容特征的方法，Recall和NDCG指标可提升一倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐中的物品冷启动问题：新添加的曲目缺乏协同过滤所需的交互历史。现有方法通常从音频、文本和元数据等内容特征学习到CF潜在空间的映射，但要么忽略艺术家信息，要么将其仅视为另一种输入模态，忽略了艺术家和物品之间的基本层次结构。

Method: 提出ACARec模型，将冷启动曲目推荐框架化为"半冷启动"，利用艺术家级别的丰富协作信号。采用基于注意力的架构，通过关注艺术家现有目录来生成新曲目的CF嵌入。

Result: 艺术家感知方法相比仅使用内容的基线方法，Recall和NDCG指标可提升一倍以上。ACARec在预测用户对新曲目的偏好方面具有显著优势，特别是在新艺术家发现和更准确估计冷物品流行度方面。

Conclusion: 通过利用艺术家级别的协作信号，可以有效解决音乐推荐中的物品冷启动问题。ACARec模型通过注意力机制关注艺术家现有目录，能够显著提升新曲目推荐的性能，特别是在新艺术家发现和流行度预测方面。

Abstract: The item cold-start problem poses a fundamental challenge for music recommendation: newly added tracks lack the interaction history that collaborative filtering (CF) requires. Existing approaches often address this problem by learning mappings from content features such as audio, text, and metadata to the CF latent space. However, previous works either omit artist information or treat it as just another input modality, missing the fundamental hierarchy of artists and items. Since most new tracks come from artists with previous history available, we frame cold-start track recommendation as 'semi-cold' by leveraging the rich collaborative signal that exists at the artist level. We show that artist-aware methods can more than double Recall and NDCG compared to content-only baselines, and propose ACARec, an attention-based architecture that generates CF embeddings for new tracks by attending over the artist's existing catalog. We show that our approach has notable advantages in predicting user preferences for new tracks, especially for new artist discovery and more accurate estimation of cold item popularity.

</details>


### [173] [BRIDGE: Multimodal-to-Text Retrieval via Reinforcement-Learned Query Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07201)
*Mohamed Darwish Mounis,Mohamed Mahmoud,Shaimaa Sedek,Mahmoud Abdalla,Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BRIDGE系统通过FORGE查询对齐模型和LENS推理增强检索器，解决多模态到文本检索中的查询对齐瓶颈，在MM-BRIGHT上超越多模态编码器和文本检索器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索系统在处理图像-文本查询对纯文本语料库时表现不佳，作者认为瓶颈不在于检索器而在于查询本身——原始多模态查询将视觉描述、对话噪声和检索意图纠缠在一起，系统性地降低了嵌入相似性

Method: 提出BRIDGE两组件系统：1) FORGE（专注检索查询生成器）——通过强化学习训练的查询对齐模型，将噪声多模态查询提炼为紧凑、检索优化的搜索字符串；2) LENS（语言增强神经搜索）——在推理密集型检索数据上微调的推理增强密集检索器，处理FORGE产生的意图丰富查询

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT（2,803个查询，29个领域）上，BRIDGE达到29.7 nDCG@10，超越所有多模态编码器基线（包括Nomic-Vision的27.6）。当FORGE作为即插即用对齐器应用于Nomic-Vision时，组合系统达到33.3 nDCG@10，超过最佳文本检索器（32.2）

Conclusion: 查询对齐是多模态到文本检索的关键瓶颈，通过专门的查询对齐模型（FORGE）和推理增强检索器（LENS）可以有效解决这一问题，无需依赖多模态编码器

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval systems struggle to resolve image-text queries against text-only corpora: the best vision-language encoder achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, underperforming strong text-only retrievers. We argue the bottleneck is not the retriever but the query -- raw multimodal queries entangle visual descriptions, conversational noise, and retrieval intent in ways that systematically degrade embedding similarity. We present \textbf{BRIDGE}, a two-component system that resolves this mismatch without multimodal encoders. \textbf{FORGE} (\textbf{F}ocused Retrieval Query Generato\textbf{r}) is a query alignment model trained via reinforcement learning, which distills noisy multimodal queries into compact, retrieval-optimized search strings. \textbf{LENS} (\textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{N}eural \textbf{S}earch) is a reasoning-enhanced dense retriever fine-tuned on reasoning-intensive retrieval data to handle the intent-rich queries FORGE produces. Evaluated on MM-BRIGHT (2,803 queries, 29 domains), BRIDGE achieves \textbf{29.7} nDCG@10, surpassing all multimodal encoder baselines including Nomic-Vision (27.6). When FORGE is applied as a plug-and-play aligner on top of Nomic-Vision, the combined system reaches \textbf{33.3} nDCG@10 -- exceeding the best text-only retriever (32.2) -- demonstrating that \textit{query alignment} is the key bottleneck in multimodal-to-text retrieval. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


### [174] [HIVE: Query, Hypothesize, Verify An LLM Framework for Multimodal Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07220)
*Mahmoud Abdalla,Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Mohamed Mahmoud,Mostafa Farouk Senussi,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HIVE框架通过LLM驱动的视觉假设生成和验证，显著提升多模态检索在推理密集型查询上的性能，在MM-BRIGHT基准上达到41.7 nDCG@10的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态检索模型在处理需要深度整合图像（图表、截图）与文本的推理密集型查询时表现不佳，在MM-BRIGHT基准上最佳多模态模型仅27.6 nDCG@10，甚至不如纯文本检索器（32.2）。

Method: 提出HIVE框架：1）初始检索；2）LLM基于top-k候选的视觉和逻辑差距生成补偿性查询；3）使用精炼查询进行二次检索；4）LLM对候选集进行验证和重排序。

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT多模态到文本检索任务上，HIVE达到41.7 nDCG@10，比最佳文本模型提升+9.5点，比最佳多模态模型提升+14.1点，在视觉需求高的领域表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: LLM介导的视觉假设生成和验证能显著缩小多模态检索中的推理差距，HIVE框架兼容标准检索器和推理增强检索器，为多模态检索提供了有效的即插即用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval models fail on reasoning-intensive queries where images (diagrams, charts, screenshots) must be deeply integrated with text to identify relevant documents -- the best multimodal model achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, underperforming even strong text-only retrievers (32.2). We introduce \textbf{HIVE} (\textbf{H}ypothesis-driven \textbf{I}terative \textbf{V}isual \textbf{E}vidence Retrieval), a plug-and-play framework that injects explicit visual-text reasoning into a retriever via LLMs. HIVE operates in four stages: (1) initial retrieval over the corpus, (2) LLM-based compensatory query synthesis that explicitly articulates visual and logical gaps observed in top-$k$ candidates, (3) secondary retrieval with the refined query, and (4) LLM verification and reranking over the union of candidates. Evaluated on the multimodal-to-text track of MM-BRIGHT (2,803 real-world queries across 29 technical domains), HIVE achieves a new state-of-the-art aggregated nDCG@10 of \textbf{41.7} -- a \textbf{+9.5} point gain over the best text-only model (DiVeR: 32.2) and \textbf{+14.1} over the best multimodal model (Nomic-Vision: 27.6), where our reasoning-enhanced base retriever contributes 33.2 and the HIVE framework adds a further \textbf{+8.5} points -- with particularly strong results in visually demanding domains (Gaming: 68.2, Chemistry: 42.5, Sustainability: 49.4). Compatible with both standard and reasoning-enhanced retrievers, HIVE demonstrates that LLM-mediated visual hypothesis generation and verification can substantially close the multimodal reasoning gap in retrieval. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [A Benchmark of Classical and Deep Learning Models for Agricultural Commodity Price Forecasting on A Novel Bangladeshi Market Price Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06227)
*Tashreef Muhammad,Tahsin Ahmed,Meherun Farzana,Md. Mahmudul Hasan,Abrar Eyasir,Md. Emon Khan,Mahafuzul Islam Shawon,Ferdous Mondol,Mahmudul Hasan,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文介绍了AgriPriceBD数据集，评估了7种预测方法在孟加拉国5种农产品价格预测上的表现，发现不同商品的预测性差异显著，朴素持续法在某些商品上最优，Prophet和Informer等先进模型表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南亚地区缺乏用于农业商品价格预测的机器学习就绪数据集，而准确的短期价格预测对发展中国家粮食安全规划和小农户收入稳定至关重要。

Method: 1) 通过LLM辅助数字化流程创建AgriPriceBD数据集（5种商品，1779个日零售中间价）；2) 评估7种预测方法：朴素持续法、SARIMA、Prophet、BiLSTM、Transformer、Time2Vec增强Transformer、Informer；3) 使用Diebold-Mariano统计显著性检验。

Result: 商品价格可预测性存在根本异质性：朴素持续法在近似随机游走商品上占优；Time2Vec编码无统计显著优势且在青辣椒上导致灾难性退化（MAE增加146.1%）；Prophet系统性失败；Informer产生不稳定预测（方差达真实值50倍）。

Conclusion: 农业商品价格预测需考虑商品特异性，简单模型有时优于复杂模型；稀疏注意力Transformer需要更大训练集；公开代码、模型和数据支持孟加拉国及类似发展中经济体农业商品市场预测研究。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of agricultural commodity prices is critical for food security planning and smallholder income stabilisation in developing economies, yet machine-learning-ready datasets for this purpose remain scarce in South Asia. This paper makes two contributions. First, we introduce AgriPriceBD, a benchmark dataset of 1,779 daily retail mid-prices for five Bangladeshi commodities - garlic, chickpea, green chilli, cucumber, and sweet pumpkin - spanning July 2020 to June 2025, extracted from government reports via an LLM-assisted digitisation pipeline. Second, we evaluate seven forecasting approaches spanning classical models - naïve persistence, SARIMA, and Prophet - and deep learning architectures - BiLSTM, Transformer, Time2Vec-enhanced Transformer, and Informer - with Diebold-Mariano statistical significance tests. Commodity price forecastability is fundamentally heterogeneous: naïve persistence dominates on near-random-walk commodities. Time2Vec temporal encoding provides no statistically significant advantage over fixed sinusoidal encoding and causes catastrophic degradation on green chilli (+146.1% MAE, p<0.001). Prophet fails systematically, attributable to discrete step-function price dynamics incompatible with its smooth decomposition assumptions. Informer produces erratic predictions (variance up to 50x ground-truth), confirming sparse-attention Transformers require substantially larger training sets than small agricultural datasets provide. All code, models, and data are released publicly to support replication and future forecasting research on agricultural commodity markets in Bangladesh and similar developing economies.

</details>


### [176] [FLeX: Fourier-based Low-rank EXpansion for multilingual transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06253)
*Gaurav Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA微调结合Sophia优化器和傅里叶正则化，在小型高质量数据集上实现了从Python到Java的跨语言代码生成性能提升，超越了传统全参数微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中多种编程语言共存，需要对大语言模型进行跨语言代码生成。传统为每种语言单独微调大模型计算成本过高，需要探索参数高效微调方法。

Method: 使用Code Llama 7B模型，采用LoRA（低秩适应）参数高效微调方法，比较Adam和Sophia优化器，并探索基于傅里叶变换的正则化技术。在MBPP数据集上进行微调，评估从Python到Java的跨语言迁移性能。

Result: 1) LoRA微调在小型高质量数据集上超越了广泛微调的Code Llama-Python-7B模型（40.1% vs 38.4% pass@1）；2) Sophia优化器收敛更快但最终性能与Adam差异不大；3) 傅里叶正则化显著提升跨语言迁移，Java任务pass@1达到42.1%，相比基线34.2%有显著提升。

Conclusion: 结合LoRA、优化训练方法和频域正则化，可以高效地将单语言大语言模型适配到多种编程语言，为跨语言代码生成提供了计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-lingual code generation is critical in enterprise environments where multiple programming languages coexist. However, fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) individually for each language is computationally prohibitive. This paper investigates whether parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods and optimizer enhancements can improve cross-lingual transfer from Python to languages like Java. We fine-tune the Code Llama 7B model using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to optimize a small subset of parameters and compare Adam and Sophia optimizers, while exploring a novel Fourier-based regularization technique. Our contributions include: (1)demonstrating that LoRA fine-tuning on a small, high-quality dataset (MBPP) can exceed the pass@1 performance of the more broadly fine-tuned Code Llama-Python-7B model (40.1% vs. 38.4%); (2) showing that while Sophia achieves faster convergence than Adam, final pass@1 scores show marginal differences; and (3) presenting evidence that Fourier-based regularization during fine-tuning significantly improves cross-lingual transfer, achieving 42.1% pass@1 on Java tasks compared to the 34.2% baseline. These findings suggest that combining LoRA, optimized training methods, and frequency-domain regularization can efficiently adapt single-language LLMs to perform well across multiple programming languages.

</details>


### [177] [Spectral Edge Dynamics Reveal Functional Modes of Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06256)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现训练动态中的"谱边缘"方向揭示了任务的功能模式，这些方向无法通过标准机制可解释性工具捕获，其结构取决于任务的代数对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究训练动态中的"谱边缘"现象，探索标准机制可解释性工具（如头归因、激活探测、稀疏自编码器）为何无法捕获这些方向，并理解这些方向如何反映任务的代数结构。

Method: 通过分析不同数学任务（模加法、乘法、减法、x²+y²）的训练动态，研究谱边缘方向的功能结构。使用傅里叶分析、离散对数基变换等方法，比较不同任务中谱边缘方向的特征。

Result: 1. 模加法中所有主导方向坍缩为单一傅里叶模式；2. 乘法中仅在离散对数基中出现类似坍缩，浓度提高5.9倍；3. 减法中谱边缘跨越小型多模式族；4. x²+y²任务中无单一谐波基足够，但加性和乘性特征的交叉项提供4倍方差提升；5. 多任务训练增强组合结构，x²+y²谱边缘继承加法电路特征频率（浓度提高2.3倍）。

Conclusion: 训练发现输入域上的低维功能模式，其结构取决于任务的代数对称性。简单的谐波结构仅当任务允许对称适应基时出现；更复杂的任务需要更丰富的功能描述。谱边缘动态识别了控制学习的低维功能子空间。

Abstract: Training dynamics during grokking concentrate along a small number of dominant update directions -- the spectral edge -- which reliably distinguishes grokking from non-grokking regimes. We show that standard mechanistic interpretability tools (head attribution, activation probing, sparse autoencoders) fail to capture these directions: their structure is not localized in parameter or feature space. Instead, each direction induces a structured function over the input domain, revealing low-dimensional functional modes invisible to representation-level analysis.
  For modular addition, all leading directions collapse to a single Fourier mode. For multiplication, the same collapse appears only in the discrete-log basis, yielding a 5.9x improvement in concentration. For subtraction, the edge spans a small multi-mode family. For $x^2+y^2$, no single harmonic basis suffices, but cross-terms of additive and multiplicative features provide a 4x variance boost, consistent with the decomposition (a+b)^2 - 2ab. Multitask training amplifies this compositional structure, with the $x^2+y^2$ spectral edge inheriting the addition circuit's characteristic frequency (2.3x concentration increase).
  These results suggest that training discovers low-dimensional functional modes over the input domain, whose structure depends on the algebraic symmetry of the task.
  These results suggest that spectral edge dynamics identify low-dimensional functional subspaces governing learning, whose representation depends on the algebraic structure of the task. Simple harmonic structure emerges only when the task admits a symmetry-adapted basis; more complex tasks require richer functional descriptions.

</details>


### [178] [MO-RiskVAE: A Multi-Omics Variational Autoencoder for Survival Risk Modeling in Multiple MyelomaMO-RiskVAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06267)
*Zixuan Chen,Heng Zhang,YuPeng Qin,WenPeng Xing,Qiang Wang,Da Wang,Changting Lin,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了多模态变分自编码器在生存风险建模中的潜在空间设计选择，发现生存驱动训练主要对正则化幅度和结构敏感，而非特定散度公式。基于此提出了MO-RiskVAE模型，在多发性骨髓瘤风险分层中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态VAE在多发性骨髓瘤生存风险建模中表现出色，但标准潜在正则化策略在生存监督训练下往往无法保留预后相关变异，导致表示不稳定或过度约束。尽管有多种变体提出，但尚不清楚潜在设计的哪些方面在这种设置下从根本上影响性能。

Method: 在统一的MyeVAE框架扩展中，系统隔离了正则化尺度、后验几何和潜在空间结构，在相同架构和优化协议下进行对照研究。特别研究了KL正则化、MMD和HSIC等散度机制，以及基于Gumbel-Softmax的混合连续-离散潜在空间结构。

Result: 生存驱动训练主要对潜在正则化的幅度和结构敏感，而非特定散度公式。适度放松KL正则化能持续改善生存区分度，而MMD和HSIC等替代机制在没有适当缩放时效果有限。结构化潜在空间能改善学习表示与生存风险梯度之间的对齐，Gumbel-Softmax混合连续-离散公式增强了连续潜在子空间中的全局风险排序。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现实例化了鲁棒的多模态生存模型MO-RiskVAE，该模型在不引入额外监督或复杂训练启发式的情况下，持续改善了多发性骨髓瘤的风险分层效果，优于原始MyeVAE。

Abstract: Multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) have emerged as a powerful framework for survival risk modeling in multiple myeloma by integrating heterogeneous omics and clinical data. However, when trained under survival supervision, standard latent regularization strategies often fail to preserve prognostically relevant variation, leading to unstable or overly constrained representations. Despite numerous proposed variants, it remains unclear which aspects of latent design fundamentally govern performance in this setting. In this work, we conduct a controlled investigation of latent modeling choices for multimodal survival prediction within a unified extension of the MyeVAE framework. By systematically isolating regularization scale, posterior geometry, and latent space structure under identical architectures and optimization protocols, we show that survival-driven training is primarily sensitive to the magnitude and structure of latent regularization rather than the specific divergence formulation. In particular, moderate relaxation of KL regularization consistently improves survival discrimination, while alternative divergence mechanisms such as MMD and HSIC provide limited benefit without appropriate scaling. We further demonstrate that structuring the latent space can improve alignment between learned representations and survival risk gradients. A hybrid continuous--discrete formulation based on Gumbel--Softmax enhances global risk ordering in the continuous latent subspace, even though stable discrete subtype discovery does not emerge under survival supervision. Guided by these findings, we instantiate a robust multimodal survival model, termed MO-RiskVAE, which consistently improves risk stratification over the original MyeVAE without introducing additional supervision or complex training heuristics.

</details>


### [179] [TalkLoRA: Communication-Aware Mixture of Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06291)
*Lin Mu,Haiyang Wang,Li Ni,Lei Sang,Zhize Wu,Peiquan Jin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TalkLoRA：一种通信感知的MoE-LoRA框架，通过专家间通信改善路由稳定性，提升参数高效微调性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE增强的LoRA方法假设专家独立运行，导致路由不稳定和专家主导问题。需要打破专家独立性假设，通过专家间通信改善路由决策。

Method: 提出TalkLoRA框架，引入专家级通信机制：1）在路由前添加轻量级Talking Module实现专家子空间间的受控信息交换；2）生成更鲁棒的全局信号用于路由；3）理论上证明通信能平滑路由动态并缓解扰动放大。

Result: 在多种语言理解和生成任务上，TalkLoRA持续优于原始LoRA和MoELoRA，在相同参数预算下实现更高的参数效率和更平衡的专家路由。

Conclusion: 结构化专家通信是MoE基参数高效适应的原则性和有效增强，TalkLoRA为大型语言模型的参数高效微调提供了更稳定和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs), and recent Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) extensions further enhance flexibility by dynamically combining multiple LoRA experts. However, existing MoE-augmented LoRA methods assume that experts operate independently, often leading to unstable routing, expert dominance. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TalkLoRA}, a communication-aware MoELoRA framework that relaxes this independence assumption by introducing expert-level communication prior to routing. TalkLoRA equips low-rank experts with a lightweight Talking Module that enables controlled information exchange across expert subspaces, producing a more robust global signal for routing. Theoretically, we show that expert communication smooths routing dynamics by mitigating perturbation amplification while strictly generalizing existing MoELoRA architectures. Empirically, TalkLoRA consistently outperforms vanilla LoRA and MoELoRA across diverse language understanding and generation tasks, achieving higher parameter efficiency and more balanced expert routing under comparable parameter budgets. These results highlight structured expert communication as a principled and effective enhancement for MoE-based parameter-efficient adaptation. Code is available at https://github.com/why0129/TalkLoRA.

</details>


### [180] [Drifting Fields are not Conservative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06333)
*Leonard Franz,Sebastian Hoffmann,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 漂移模型通过向量值漂移场将生成样本向数据分布传输，但研究发现漂移场通常不是保守场（不能表示为标量势的梯度），只有高斯核是例外。论文提出通过锐化核的替代归一化方法恢复保守性，并建议使用更简单的损失函数训练漂移模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究漂移模型是否等价于优化标量损失，因为漂移模型通过向量值漂移场传输样本，理论上可能比标量损失优化更灵活，但需要验证其数学等价性和实际价值。

Method: 1. 分析漂移场的保守性：证明一般漂移场不是保守场，不能表示为标量势的梯度；2. 识别非保守性来源：位置相关的归一化；3. 发现高斯核是唯一例外；4. 提出锐化核归一化方法，为任意径向核恢复保守性；5. 比较漂移场匹配目标与损失最小化的实际效果。

Result: 1. 漂移场通常是非保守的，只有高斯核情况下才是保守的；2. 锐化核归一化方法能恢复漂移场的保守性，从而定义明确的损失函数；3. 虽然漂移场匹配目标理论上更通用（可实现非保守传输场），但实际增益有限；4. 建议使用基于损失函数的更简单训练方法。

Conclusion: 漂移模型的漂移场匹配目标在理论上比标量损失最小化更通用，但实际应用中这种灵活性带来的收益有限。因此推荐使用基于损失函数的更简单训练方案，这既保持了理论严谨性又简化了实现。

Abstract: Drifting models generate high-quality samples in a single forward pass by transporting generated samples toward the data distribution using a vector valued drift field. We investigate whether this procedure is equivalent to optimizing a scalar loss and find that, in general, it is not: drift fields are not conservative - they cannot be written as the gradient of any scalar potential. We identify the position-dependent normalization as the source of non-conservatism. The Gaussian kernel is the unique exception where the normalization is harmless and the drift field is exactly the gradient of a scalar function. Generalizing this, we propose an alternative normalization via a related kernel (the sharp kernel) which restores conservatism for any radial kernel, yielding well-defined loss functions for training drifting models. While we identify that the drifting field matching objective is strictly more general than loss minimization, as it can implement non-conservative transport fields that no scalar loss can reproduce, we observe that practical gains obtained utilizing this flexibility are minimal. We thus propose to train drifting models with the conceptually simpler formulations utilizing loss functions.

</details>


### [181] [BiScale-GTR: Fragment-Aware Graph Transformers for Multi-Scale Molecular Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06336)
*Yi Yang,Ovidiu Daescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiScale-GTR：结合化学片段分词与自适应多尺度推理的自监督分子表示学习框架，在多个基准测试中实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图Transformer方法主要受GNN主导，表示仍受局部消息传递影响，且大多仅操作单一结构粒度，难以捕获跨多个分子尺度的模式

Method: 改进图Byte Pair Encoding分词生成一致、化学有效、高覆盖率的片段token；采用并行GNN-Transformer架构，将GNN学习的原子级表示池化为片段级嵌入并与片段token嵌入融合，再进行Transformer推理

Result: 在MoleculeNet、PharmaBench和LRGB基准测试中，在分类和回归任务上均达到最先进的性能；归因分析显示模型能突出化学有意义的官能团

Conclusion: BiScale-GTR通过结合化学片段分词与自适应多尺度推理，有效捕获局部化学环境、子结构级基序和长程分子依赖，为分子表示学习提供了统一框架

Abstract: Graph Transformers have recently attracted attention for molecular property prediction by combining the inductive biases of graph neural networks (GNNs) with the global receptive field of Transformers. However, many existing hybrid architectures remain GNN-dominated, causing the resulting representations to remain heavily shaped by local message passing. Moreover, most existing methods operate at only a single structural granularity, limiting their ability to capture molecular patterns that span multiple molecular scales. We introduce BiScale-GTR, a unified framework for self-supervised molecular representation learning that combines chemically grounded fragment tokenization with adaptive multi-scale reasoning. Our method improves graph Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) tokenization to produce consistent, chemically valid, and high-coverage fragment tokens, which are used as fragment-level inputs to a parallel GNN-Transformer architecture. Architecturally, atom-level representations learned by a GNN are pooled into fragment-level embeddings and fused with fragment token embeddings before Transformer reasoning, enabling the model to jointly capture local chemical environments, substructure-level motifs, and long-range molecular dependencies. Experiments on MoleculeNet, PharmaBench, and the Long Range Graph Benchmark (LRGB) demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across both classification and regression tasks. Attribution analysis further shows that BiScale-GTR highlights chemically meaningful functional motifs, providing interpretable links between molecular structure and predicted properties. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [182] [The Master Key Hypothesis: Unlocking Cross-Model Capability Transfer via Linear Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06377)
*Rishab Balasubramanian,Pin-Jie Lin,Rituraj Sharma,Anjie Fang,Fardin Abdi,Viktor Rozgic,Zheng Du,Mohit Bansal,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UNLOCK框架无需训练即可在不同规模模型间迁移后训练能力，通过提取能力方向并在低维潜空间中对齐实现能力转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后训练能力是否可以在不同规模的模型之间迁移而无需重新训练，探索模型能力在潜空间中的可转移性。

Method: 提出Master Key假设，认为模型能力对应低维潜空间中的方向，可通过线性对齐在不同模型间转移。UNLOCK框架：1）通过对比具备能力和不具备能力的源模型变体的激活来提取能力方向；2）通过低秩线性变换将方向与目标模型对齐；3）在推理时应用该方向以激发行为。

Result: 在推理行为（包括思维链和数学推理）上的实验显示，无需训练即可在不同模型规模间实现显著改进：将CoT推理从Qwen1.5-14B迁移到Qwen1.5-7B，在MATH数据集上准确率提升12.1%；将数学推理方向从Qwen3-4B-Base迁移到Qwen3-14B-Base，AGIEval Math准确率从61.1%提升到71.3%，超过14B后训练模型的67.8%。

Conclusion: 模型能力的成功转移依赖于预训练期间学习的能力，UNLOCK干预通过锐化输出分布朝向成功的推理轨迹来放大潜在能力，证明了后训练能力在不同规模模型间的可转移性。

Abstract: We investigate whether post-trained capabilities can be transferred across models without retraining, with a focus on transfer across different model scales. We propose the Master Key Hypothesis, which states that model capabilities correspond to directions in a low-dimensional latent subspace that induce specific behaviors and are transferable across models through linear alignment. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce UNLOCK, a training-free and label-free framework that extracts a capability direction by contrasting activations between capability-present and capability-absent Source variants, aligns it with a Target model through a low-rank linear transformation, and applies it at inference time to elicit the behavior. Experiments on reasoning behaviors, including Chain-of-Thought (CoT) and mathematical reasoning, demonstrate substantial improvements across model scales without training. For example, transferring CoT reasoning from Qwen1.5-14B to Qwen1.5-7B yields an accuracy gain of 12.1% on MATH, and transferring a mathematical reasoning direction from Qwen3-4B-Base to Qwen3-14B-Base improves AGIEval Math accuracy from 61.1% to 71.3%, surpassing the 67.8% achieved by the 14B post-trained model. Our analysis shows that the success of transfer depends on the capabilities learned during pre-training, and that our intervention amplifies latent capabilities by sharpening the output distribution toward successful reasoning trajectories.

</details>


### [183] [Neural Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06425)
*Mingchen Zhuge,Changsheng Zhao,Haozhe Liu,Zijian Zhou,Shuming Liu,Wenyi Wang,Ernie Chang,Gael Le Lan,Junjie Fei,Wenxuan Zhang,Yasheng Sun,Zhipeng Cai,Zechun Liu,Yunyang Xiong,Yining Yang,Yuandong Tian,Yangyang Shi,Vikas Chandra,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经计算机新范式，将模型本身作为运行计算机，统一计算、内存和I/O于学习到的运行时状态中


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越传统计算机、智能体和世界模型的局限，探索将模型本身作为运行计算机的新计算范式，实现完全神经计算机的长期目标

Method: 从收集的I/O轨迹中学习早期NC原语，实例化为视频模型，在CLI和GUI环境中从指令、像素和用户动作生成屏幕帧

Result: 学习到的运行时可以获得早期接口原语，特别是I/O对齐和短时程控制，但常规重用、受控更新和符号稳定性仍是开放问题

Conclusion: 神经计算机代表超越当前计算范式的新前沿，完全神经计算机的实现需要解决多个挑战，但可能建立全新的计算范式

Abstract: We propose a new frontier: Neural Computers (NCs) -- an emerging machine form that unifies computation, memory, and I/O in a learned runtime state. Unlike conventional computers, which execute explicit programs, agents, which act over external execution environments, and world models, which learn environment dynamics, NCs aim to make the model itself the running computer. Our long-term goal is the Completely Neural Computer (CNC): the mature, general-purpose realization of this emerging machine form, with stable execution, explicit reprogramming, and durable capability reuse. As an initial step, we study whether early NC primitives can be learned solely from collected I/O traces, without instrumented program state. Concretely, we instantiate NCs as video models that roll out screen frames from instructions, pixels, and user actions (when available) in CLI and GUI settings. These implementations show that learned runtimes can acquire early interface primitives, especially I/O alignment and short-horizon control, while routine reuse, controlled updates, and symbolic stability remain open. We outline a roadmap toward CNCs around these challenges. If overcome, CNCs could establish a new computing paradigm beyond today's agents, world models, and conventional computers.

</details>


### [184] [The Depth Ceiling: On the Limits of Large Language Models in Discovering Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06427)
*Yi Xu,Philipp Jettkant,Laura Ruis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs在潜在推理方面存在显著限制：即使经过大规模缩放，模型最多只能发现和执行有限步数的潜在规划策略，这为链式思维监控提供了理论支持


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维监控的有效性依赖于模型无法在其潜在表示中进行有效推理，但目前对LLMs潜在推理能力的极限知之甚少，需要系统研究模型在无中间步骤监督的情况下发现和执行多步规划策略的能力

Method: 使用图路径查找任务精确控制所需潜在规划步数，测试不同规模模型：从头训练的小型Transformer、微调的GPT-4o和Qwen3-32B、few-shot提示的GPT-5.4，评估它们发现和执行潜在规划策略的能力

Result: 模型能力存在明显限制：小型Transformer最多发现3步潜在规划，微调GPT-4o和Qwen3-32B达到5步，GPT-5.4在few-shot下达到7步。训练时最大潜在规划深度为5步，但发现的策略在测试时可泛化到8步，表明策略发现与执行能力存在分离

Conclusion: LLMs在潜在多步规划方面存在根本性限制，需要多个协调潜在规划步骤的策略可能需要显式教学或外部化，这为链式思维监控提供了实证支持，表明复杂推理可能需要外部化思维过程

Abstract: The viability of chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring hinges on models being unable to reason effectively in their latent representations. Yet little is known about the limits of such latent reasoning in LLMs. We test these limits by studying whether models can discover multi-step planning strategies without supervision on intermediate steps and execute them latently, within a single forward pass. Using graph path-finding tasks that precisely control the number of required latent planning steps, we uncover a striking limitation unresolved by massive scaling: tiny transformers trained from scratch discover strategies requiring up to three latent steps, fine-tuned GPT-4o and Qwen3-32B reach five, and GPT-5.4 attains seven under few-shot prompting. Although the maximum latent planning depth models can learn during training is five, the discovered strategy generalizes up to eight latent steps at test-time. This reveals a dissociation between the ability to discover a latent strategy under final-answer supervision alone and the ability to execute it once discovered. If similar limits hold more broadly, strategies requiring multiple coordinated latent planning steps may need to be explicitly taught or externalized, lending credence to CoT monitoring.

</details>


### [185] [Quality-preserving Model for Electronics Production Quality Tests Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06451)
*Noufa Haneefa,Teddy Lazebnik,Einav Peretz-Andersson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应测试选择框架，结合离线最小成本诊断子集构建与在线Thompson采样多臂老虎机，在PCB组装测试中动态切换完整与精简测试计划，显著降低测试时间同时控制缺陷逃逸风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高产量电子产品制造测试流程在产品开发阶段固定，即使故障模式和工艺条件变化也不调整，这保证了质量但增加了不必要的测试成本。现有数据驱动方法主要优化静态测试子集，无法在线适应变化的缺陷分布，也不能明确控制逃逸风险。

Method: 提出自适应测试选择框架：1) 离线阶段使用贪心集合覆盖构建最小成本诊断子集；2) 在线阶段采用Thompson采样多臂老虎机，基于滚动过程稳定性信号在完整测试计划和精简测试计划之间切换。

Result: 在PCB组装两个阶段（功能电路测试和终线测试）28,000块电路板上评估：离线分析识别出零逃逸精简计划，功能电路测试时间减少18.78%，终线测试减少91.57%。在时间验证中，静态精简在功能电路测试产生110个逃逸缺陷，终线测试8个；自适应策略通过在不稳定时恢复完整覆盖，将逃逸降至零。

Conclusion: 在线学习能在降低测试负担的同时保持制造质量，为跨生产领域的自适应测试规划提供实用途径，为企业带来经济和物流改进。

Abstract: Manufacturing test flows in high-volume electronics production are typically fixed during product development and executed unchanged on every unit, even as failure patterns and process conditions evolve. This protects quality, but it also imposes unnecessary test cost, while existing data-driven methods mostly optimize static test subsets and neither adapt online to changing defect distributions nor explicitly control escape risk. In this study, we present an adaptive test-selection framework that combines offline minimum-cost diagnostic subset construction using greedy set cover with an online Thompson-sampling multi-armed bandit that switches between full and reduced test plans using a rolling process-stability signal. We evaluate the framework on two printed circuit board assembly stages-Functional Circuit Test and End-of-Line test-covering 28,000 board runs. Offline analysis identified zero-escape reduced plans that cut test time by 18.78% in Functional Circuit Test and 91.57\% in End-of-Line testing.
